> **Earlier — FUSED ZAMASU, EPISODES 64–67: "VEGITO" IN THE EPISODES' ORDER; THE SAIYAN ASSAULT; THE SPIRIT SWORD EXCHANGE; THE MUTATION; THE FINAL KAMEHAMEHA PLAYED; TRUNKS' BLOCK (2026-10-19). Start here.** Details: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.30**; research: `documentation/FUSED_ZAMASU_EPISODES_64_67.md`.
> - **Research:** the research PDF, the Kanzenshuu guides 64–67, Wikipedia, the ANN review of 65. A 23-beat chronology, kept apart from the owner's recollections and the adaptations. It changed the mission: the combined assault belongs in episode 65, before Trunks' Galick Gun (the old charge after the halo beat is gone). He changes to the Half-Corrupted body with his tears after the Spirit Sword, and he survives the Final Kamehameha. Trunks' block was missing.
> - **"Vegito" phases are now `merge, assault, turn, fight, wrath, fight2, final`.**
>   - **ASSAULT** (new, played; switchable Goku/Vegeta): the Blades of Judgement and a lockable Lightning Bird over the god. Then an establishing shot and the cue: **the Saiyan Assault**, a real team rush. The blows alternate from both sides; the spinning kick and the axe kick are slowed and time is given back; the mission's lens follows the hits, and Zamasu reacts to each. **He catches the last double blow and throws them** (the episode's outcome). The blows are an ADAPTATION of the owner's memory.
>   - **TURN:** episodes 65–66 in order. The Kamehameha against the gigantic Holy Wrath is a mash struggle. **"Alright!" is untouched**, as are the held Blue and the two fingers.
>   - **WRATH:** the Spirit Sword is a timing exchange. The prompt shows only in the last 30 frames of his lunge; a miss lets his blade land and he comes again, and after three misses the counter comes anyway.
>   - **FIGHT 2:** opens with his tears and **the Half-Corrupted change**; he then grows as his bar falls.
>   - **FINAL:** the taunt, then **the charge held (mash)** while he comes down at Vegito, then **"Release it!" (Ultimate)**. The split follows; he is still standing and Goku is the player.
> - **"The Sword of Hope":** it now opens on **Trunks blocking the god's blade** over the spent Goku and Vegeta.
> - **Free Battle: Saiyan Assault** (Goku, Blue, with Blue Vegeta). It is earned with "Vegito", or with The Final Battle on an old save.
> - **Fixed along the way:**
>   - `K.judgment` counted the bird's marks (they share its delay), so a skipped run stalled. The fix: telegraphs now name their caster.
>   - Browser fixes: the bird was off-frame and not lockable; the establishing shot; the axe-kick framing; stones across the lens in Blue and in the mutation; a blade during the lunge; a frozen charge lens. A 999-second shot's `t` is normalized, so use `el`.

> **Earlier — NEXT BATCH, GROUP 1: THE TICK-HANDLER FIX; "THE SWORD OF HOPE" AS A MULTI-STAGE BOSS (2026-10-19). Start here.** Details: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.27**.
> - **Engine (`battle.js`):** a `b.onTick` / `b.after` registered from inside a tick handler is no longer lost; it first runs on the next tick, after the others, once. `onTick`/`after` return a stop function that is safe mid-tick. A throwing handler keeps the list. **Sibling (`mission.js`):** a mission tick hook removing itself no longer makes the next hook skip a tick.
> - **Revived by the fix (the code always asked for them):** Beerus' ki barrage in *Battle of Gods* (16 balls, craters), Blue Gogeta's sphere impacts and walk-through lava geysers (`bb_gogeta`), the barrier fade in Fusion Reborn's farewell. Bot runs watched: `bog_battle`, `bb_gogeta`, `fr_gogeta` won, 0 errors.

"Vegito"'s judgment pattern is the shared `K.judgment` (trunks_kit).

> **Historical checkpoint (2026-10-10, final polish): FUSION REBORN — PIKKON'S DEFEAT, JANEMBA'S THREAT, GOGETA'S FINISH.** The seven missions, the SSJ3 change, the counter-combo and the seven-shot Stardust Breaker are kept; this pass adds and refines:
> - **Pikkon's stand and defeat** — a new first phase of `fr_gogeta`, `pikkon` (checkpoint; the Gogeta phases move up by one in the Developer Menu: 1 · pikkon, 2 · dance, 3 · birth, 4 · dominate, 5 · finish). The insults scene is kept (`pikkonArrives`; the chips now use `FR.chip`). Then **played as Pikkon** on the open field (`pikkonStand`): his blows land on the armour and do nothing — Janemba has story armour (`storyArmor` 3, poise held full, takes `PK.taken` 4 % of the damage), is scripted to stand in it and follow him (no counterattacks, no Phantom Shift), and every heavy blow chips the plate; after `PK.blows` (6) landed blows or 22 s, a short cut-in (over Janemba's shoulder: "Nothing... My best blows, and he barely feels them." / "Then I won't win this. I only have to LAST.") and the objective changes to **hold him off for 24 s** (block, evade) while the AI takes over and escalates: blade + Phantom Shift, the Dimension Slash at 8 s, Rakshasa's Claw at 16 s (`PK.dealt` 0.26/0.33/0.40). Pikkon cannot die (floor 30 %; reaching it ends the stand early; optional objective "Still standing when the time is up"); his Thunder Flash is locked for the defeat. Then **the defeat, staged** (`pikkonFalls`): the Hurricane Kick into empty air — Janemba comes apart into cubes and re-forms behind him — the first cut barely blocked (driven back across the ground) — gone again, at his side: the guard breaks, the cleave throws him across the field (a tracked side lens; the jellybean ground bursts where he lands; he skids) — he gets up ("Not... yet...!") and fires the Thunder Flash — Janemba does not lift the sword: the beam goes into a portal in front of him and comes out of one behind Pikkon, into his back — a hit-stop and a quiet beat — face down, he tries to push up (new pose `pk_pushUp`) and collapses. **Alive** (halo on, `pikkonDown` keeps him out of every later phase). Janemba stands over him, turns toward the rock; Goku: "PIKKON!"; Pikkon: "Goku! Ordinary blows won't stop him... Whatever you two are planning — do it NOW!"; Vegeta: "The dance — NOW, while he is still out there."; Goku: "...it's our last chance."
> - **The second dance is the last chance**: Janemba is seen coming across the field behind the dancers, nearer with every matched beat (`approach`, `COMING`); every retry starts with him nearer; a miss is his blade between them (from behind now). Title: "The last chance".
> - **Veku's end** (`fr_veku` timeup): the last seconds are shown — Janemba walks the blimp down, Veku runs, trips onto his back, the sword goes up over him, and the fusion runs out under the blade (Goku and Vegeta spring apart, the blade hits the ground between them, "The rock — MOVE!"). The timeup scene opens on Janemba hunting them over the open ground and adds the realisation (Vegeta: "Forget the dance. I'll finish him myself!" — Goku: "We both tried that, Vegeta. On our own, he takes us apart one at a time." — "One more try — done right. It's the only real chance we've got."), ending on Janemba turning toward the rock (which is where `fr_gogeta` opens).
> - **Armour damage readable**: `model.chipArmor(i)` (beside `damageArmor`; repaired by `repairArmor`) lays a jagged dark crack with a pale broken flake on the chest plate at one of eight spots — no glow; `FR.chip` adds the moment (plate fragments in the armour's own dark and pale tones falling away, a violet ring, a crack of sound).
> - **SSJ3 glass orbs** (`fr_janemba` ssj3Film): bigger (1.3–2.1 m), closer (10–16 m), a dark blue rim (reads on the bright candy sky) and a hard white highlight (reads on dark); the shatters moved into the shots that see the sky behind him (scream: frames 82–136; silence: 240; the rest at the burst), each with a ring and big falling shards in pale and dark blues — no white flash.
> - **The dust's flight** (Breaker G3/G4): one readable comet (a bright heart, solid specks of every colour, a rainbow streak, and a soft dark bed under it so the colours stand off bright ground as well as dark); 50 frames instead of 42 (no slow motion); two lenses — riding backward ahead of it, low at his chest height and tilting up after it, so it rises over him against the far dark sky; then behind it and aside, chasing it into Janemba's chest. Then a **hit beat** (~0.5 s: a ring of every colour, the jolt `hitBody`, then the frozen scream) before the purification starts.
> - **The two-finger catch** (`catchFit`, `bladeGap`, exported on `SL.data.frGogeta`): solved before the stand-off's first frame — both bodies are put in their end poses for an instant, the blade point level with the fingers is found and Janemba is placed by the gap (the turns are held, then Janemba is turned half way back toward Gogeta). At the cue the blade comes down and the hand comes up (blended, not cut), and the moment the blade reaches the fingers both are set on their end poses: **Janemba is never moved at the catch** (measured < 1 cm drift; the old align hook is gone). New `gg_catchBlind` (arm out at head height, clear of the hair; head turned away) and `sj_caught` (sword arm raised to -92°): the blade meets the fingers near its point. Lenses on Gogeta's own facing: the cue shot shows the blade up over Janemba's head; the catch shows his calm face turned away and the whole blade from the demon's fist to two fingers.

New tests in `tests/fusion-reborn-tests.js`: Pikkon's stand and defeat (armoured blows that chip and do nothing, no counterattack while he is confident, down and alive, out of the dance) and the catch (blade < 5 cm from the fingers, Janemba not moved). The earlier Breaker work is kept (the seven hold-driven shots A–F, the multicolour sphere, early release, auto completion); the release and everything after it follow the beat sheet now.
> - **1 · SSJ3** (`fr_janemba` ascend): the form's own sequence shot the film's way through new `C.transform` options (`changeShots` cut list, `onFrame` beats, `free` lenses; the engine's own sequence camera is switched off with the existing `noShots`): a tight low-angle close-up on the clenched teeth → the scream (new pose `g3_scream`: elbows back to the ribs, head thrown at the sky) with blue lightning, the candy world shaking and floating glass orbs shattering behind him → the lens dropping over the brow as the eyebrows go (brows removed at frame 168, ahead of the burst) → the mane pouring down his back in slow motion → the silence → the burst. The aura is held to a glow during the change so the face reads. Canon SSJ3 (`buu_ssj3`) is untouched (no `changeShots` there).
> - **2 · Goku & Vegeta vs Super Janemba** (`fr_vegeta`): Goku fights in **Super Saiyan 2** (the beat sheet; SSJ3 is locked for this mission only, `lockForms` override — the earlier brief said "Base/SSJ/SSJ3", this is the owner's newer instruction). After the needles Goku is SEEN dragging the battered Vegeta by the arm (pose `g_drag`) before the cut behind the spiked rock; Vegeta stays down on one knee clutching his **bleeding right shoulder** through the whole proposal (pose `v_kneelShoulder`, `FR.wound`: a dark red patch and falling drops), Goku standing over him, explaining in a rush; then Super Janemba hovers high over the open ground cutting the empty air and every cut rains dimensional glass needles on the hideout (`FR.needles`) — "He found us — MOVE!" — and both leap up into the air.
> - **3 · The fusion timings**: `fr_veku` — the first dance is danced **in mid-air under fire** (Janemba overhead, needles raining past them every beat or so); the bent finger as before; Veku forms in the air, drops out of the light and bounces on the ground. `fr_gogeta` — before the second dance **Pikkon buys the time**: in Janemba's face shouting insults (original lines), every insult chips and cracks the armour, Janemba turns on him and they are gone across the sky; Goku and Vegeta dance **on solid ground**.
> - **4 · Gogeta's cold stare and the glide**: the reveal lines are delivered stone-faced (new expression `cold`); then a tight low-angle shot up at his jaw, the teal eyes looking down; then **Janemba's own point of view** as Gogeta closes in with a glide (pose `gg_glide`: legs still, toes just off the ground, 1.2 s).
> - **5 · The five-part counter-combo** (finish, five cues — Guard, Light, Heavy, Light, Heavy): the downward slash caught between two fingers **without looking** (`gg_catchBlind`; the blade is set between the fingers exactly) and snapped to glittering glass → he vanishes and drives a **high knee** into chest and chin → back turned, a **reverse heel kick** to the jaw (`gg_backKick`) → wide profile, **three shockwave fists** → turned square, the **double palm** — Janemba tumbles end over end through the air (`sj_tumble`) and Gogeta turns his back on him. The catch moved out of `dominate`, whose free fight is now against an ARMED Janemba (the old unarmed BARE moveset is gone).
> - **6 · The Breaker launch**: shots A–F are made with his back to Janemba (who hangs dazed in the air behind him, small in the frame, as in the reference); on the release the fist **crushes the sphere into glittering dust** (`gg_breakerCrush`) → **behind Janemba**, past his shoulder, as he spins round to face Gogeta far off with his back turned → Gogeta **turns on his heel** → the lens rides **backward with the flicked dust** (`gg_flick`), away from him toward Janemba → it hits dead centre in the chest. Deep space stays behind them until the hit, then thins back to the pink sky.
> - **7 · The dissolution and the ogre kid**: Janemba freezes, trying to scream (`sj_scream`); rainbow light bleeds through his skin (tint), **his eyes and his mouth** (`stardust({ leak })`); he **comes apart limb by limb** into dust that floats up into the pink sky (`stardust({ apart })`); Saike falls out of the air onto his backside, **looks around** (`so_lookAround`), **looks up at Gogeta towering over him** (Saike's own eye view; `so_lookUp`), yelps, scrambles away, and Gogeta's stone face breaks into a **warm, confident smile** (expression `warm`).

> **Historical checkpoint (2026-10-10): STARDUST BREAKER FOLLOW-UP** (owner: face readability in A and D, a throw that joins Gogeta's action to the purification, the three stale tests, keep the design). The seven-shot sequence, the multicolour sphere, early release, auto completion and the Janemba → Saike purification are unchanged in kind.
> - **A and D, the faces:** both full-body shots are now fitted numerically (feet to halo / top of the sphere fill ~90% of the frame for the lens height and field of view: A fov 44 at 3.14 m, D fov 50 at 3.12 m); the head in `gg_breakerStand` / `gg_breakerRaise` is pitched down and turned toward the low right-side lenses, so the face is seen from in front, not from under the chin. Measured at 640x360: A face 25 → 29 px, D 24 → 26 px. **Limit:** a full-body frame that must also hold the halo and the raised sphere (about 3 m tall) cannot make the face much bigger; at 1080p the same faces are ~3x larger.
> - **The throw:** over Gogeta's right shoulder — his back and halo, the sphere arcing up out of his hand, Janemba waiting at the far end, in one frame — then a cut to Janemba as it arrives, and that one shot runs on through the impact, the light swelling inside him and the start of the dust (the separate purification shot was removed); the over-the-shoulder glitter shot that follows mirrors the throw's side. Flight 48 frames (0.8 s).
> - **The cut into A:** a half-second beat on Janemba reeling after the combination (the last blow's light dies away), the stance snapped on the first frame (`anim.play(..., { snap: true })`), the aura silenced on the first frame, and **`Battle.switchPlayer(to, { quiet: true })`** (new option) so the party hand-off flash, ring and sound no longer fire on a story cut.
> - **Real bug found in the real-time review and fixed (engine):** `Mission.cue` and `Mission.rhythm` read only the player body's intent, so when a scene had handed that body to a script (`K.scripted()`, as the Gogeta combination does) a human's presses never reached the cue — every one waited out its 3 s auto. New helper `playerDown(p, key)` in mission.js also reads the button itself (`SL.Input.held`, polled every tick by `Battle.tick`) when the player's controller is not the PlayerController. Bots were unaffected (their harness answers cues itself), which is why no test saw it. Any other mission that scripts the player body around a cue or a rhythm benefits.
> - **Tests:** the three stale tests were checked one by one — all obsolete expectations, none a regression: Vegeta's SSJ is a deliberate 196-frame event (`tools/lab.js` now powers down at 212 of 240 ticks); Vegeta's forms now include the story-locked `super_vegeta` (test asserts five forms, Super Vegeta story-locked and locked at spawn); Goku's forms now continue `god`, `godki` (test asserts the chain up to SSJ3, then only the god forms, each given by `bog_ritual` / `bog_sphere`, still locked after SSJ3 is earned). New `tests/fusion-reborn-tests.js` (registered in sim-tests.js): cues answered by the real button while the body is scripted (mutation-checked: fails with the fix removed), and the Breaker hold (no charge before the arm is up, early release pauses, 5.4 s to full, a held full orb is not thrown, release throws, Janemba purified, Saike back). **Full suite: clean run after the last edit, 2026-10-10: **777 passed, 0 failed**.** `--fast` 51/0.

> - **The purification is now visibly the same light:** the sphere stays inside Janemba (`inside(0.75)`), swells until he is all light, then lets go as the stardust; `stardust()` glitter made larger (it was too small to read at distance). Shots: Janemba's front from above chest height, then over Gogeta's shoulder (halo and back in the foreground) while the glitter column rises; deep space closes (80 frames) as Saike drops and the world comes back.
> - **Engine touches:** `Fighter` model `wind` (0..1) streams the cloth (`model.js`; the sash during the gathering); `Cinema.body` counts a visible halo in a fighter's silhouette, so FULL shots no longer crop halos (affects every haloed character's full shots — Buu saga and Fusion Reborn); `Aura` with view below 0.04 (`auraViewCap = 0`) also stops its particles, sparks and lightning (`vfx.js`); Gogeta now wears a halo in the film (`fuse()`).

> **Read this first if you are a new Claude session (or a new developer).**
> Before changing anything, read `MASTER_SPEC.md`, `HANDOFF.md`, `STATUS.md` and `README.md`. Inspect the current source tree and existing build. Do not rely on previous chat history.
>
> The files in this folder are the source of truth. No chat history, memory or earlier summary outranks them. If `MASTER_SPEC.md` (what the game should become), `HANDOFF.md` (where development is and what is next), `STATUS.md` (detailed factual status and test record) and `README.md` (how to run and use it) disagree, inspect the actual source code and tests before deciding which statement is correct — then fix the document that is wrong.

**Last updated:** 2026-10-10

## Current Version

| | |
|---|---|
| **Main folder** | **Version 0.3.0 "Saiyan Saga" — in development** (`SL.VERSION = '0.3.0-dev'` in `src/core/namespace.js`; the main menu footer says *Version 0.3 (in development) — Saiyan Saga*). Not frozen. |
| **Current playable (frozen) build** | **Version 0.2.0 "Goku Showcase"** — `builds/v0.2/` (double-click its `index.html`). |
| **Previous build** | Version 0.1.0 (first playable build) — `builds/v0.1/`. |
| **Save slots** | 0.3 uses `saiyanLegacy.save.v3` (imports the newest older slot once, read-only). 0.2 uses `…save.v2`, 0.1 uses `…save.v1`. Frozen builds are never modified. |

## Current Development Phase

Version 0.3 — the Saiyan Saga expansion (owner's V0.3 brief: Chapter 2 Gohan, Kaioken progression, Saibamen, Nappa, Vegeta, Great Ape, playable Piccolo, second What-If, radial skill menu, balance investigation, `.glb` pipeline). All eleven priorities exist as **functional prototypes**; none is final quality.

After that, the owner **human-played the Bardock prologue** and reported three problems (flight descent, an overwhelming opening, no way to unlock the target). Those were investigated and fixed in code and verified by automated tests and by Claude driving the real game in a browser. **The prologue now waits for a human retest.** It must not be called polished until a human has retested it.

While the prologue retest waits, development continued with P1 (2026-09-15): the multi-enemy Saiyan Saga fights were measured with the encounter probe, which led to **boss attention** in party battles, and the missing **Snake Way** run was built as its own Chapter 2 mission. Later the same day the spec's **playable Great Ape sequence** was added to *The Saiyan Prince* (the player controls Great Ape Gohan against a tail-hunting Vegeta). Yamcha then became playable with Spirit Ball Control, and the **Namek foundation began in story order** with Vegeta's first Namek mission (*A Prince on Namek*: Cui, Dodoria). All of it is functional prototype, automated tested only.

On 2026-09-16 the prologue got its **ending cinematic** (Bardock's defeat, Frieza's Supernova, his vision of his son, a quiet smile, the planet's destruction, Kakarot's pod reaching Earth). It is a functional prototype, automated tested and checked by Claude in the browser — not seen by a human yet, so it joins the prologue retest.

**Content as of 2026-09-23 (after the What-If implementation pass):** **57 mission definitions** in `src/data/missions/` — 51 canon story missions (the Bardock prologue among them) and 6 What-If missions — across a prologue and nine chapters: Chapter 1 is 1, Chapter 2 is 3, Chapter 3 is 3, Chapter 4 (Namek) is 21, Chapter 5 (Androids) is 6, Chapter 6 (Cell) is 4, Chapter 7 (Buu: seven years later) is 3, Chapter 8 (the World Tournament) is 4 and Chapter 9 (the wizard) is 5; **113** data-driven cutscenes in `src/data/scenes/` (10 added in round 2, 2 in round 3, 8 in round 4, 4 in the finale pass); **66** characters (19 playable); **17** stages. The last full run of `node tests/run-tests.js` reported **678 passed, 0 failed** (after the character-quality pass, 2026-09-25). Everything from Chapter 4 on is a **functional prototype, automated tested**. **Human playtest boundary:** the owner has played the story from the start **through *The Warrior* (Frieza vs Nail)** (round 2, before that pass's fixes); nothing after it has been played by a person, and the round-2 fixes await the owner's retest. Chapters 4–6, Buu Milestones A and B and (since the core-feel pass) *The Mark* have been browser-verified by Claude.

**Buu Saga continued (2026-09-24):** Chapter 10 *Majin* (*The Mark* rebuilt, *Prince of Saiyans*, *Majin Buu*, *The Prince's Pride*) and the first mission of Chapter 11 (*The Third Level*): Majin Vegeta, Goku vs Majin Vegeta, Buu's awakening, Vegeta's sacrifice and Super Saiyan 3, with a four-level torn-clothing system, SSJ2/SSJ3 and Majin Buu. Now **61 missions** (55 canon, 6 What-If), **133** cutscenes, **67** characters, **18** stages. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested**.

**Kid Buu (2026-10-02, latest):** the owner's Buu Saga brief, priority 11 — Chapter 14 *Pure Evil* and *Number One* (Kid Buu, the Earth destroyed as a world state, the Sacred World of the Kai, SSJ3 as a draining resource, Vegeta's one-minute countdown, Good Buu as a playable interruption). **Buu Saga reference pass 2 — the Fusion Dance and Gotenks (2026-09-25):** the same brief's **Priority 7**, built as one slice: mission *Fusion* (Chapter 11, after *The Third Level*) on the new **Kami's Lookout** stage — Goku dances the Fusion Dance for the boys and leaves; the dance is **played as a rhythm** (`Mission.rhythm`); the first fusion comes out fat (Goten's finger), a step out of time comes out old, on the beat it is **Gotenks**; a spar with Piccolo (Super Saiyan handed over, the Super Ghost Kamikaze Attack); the thirty minutes run out mid-brag. New **`SL.Fusion`** (fuse/defuse, timer) and **Gotenks** (playable; SSJ3 defined and locked until a Time Chamber mission exists). Now **62 missions** (56 canon, 6 What-If), **163** cutscenes, **73** characters (20 playable), **20** stages. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested — HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED**. `STATUS.md` → *Buu Saga reference pass 2*.

**Buu Saga reference pass 1 — the 25th World Tournament (2026-09-25):** the owner sent a full Buu Saga brief with a visual reference board and a written chronology of the whole saga (the seven years to Uub) and a priority order (1 the Budokai … 13 the epilogue/Uub). This pass **audited the whole existing Buu Saga against both references** (gap report: `STATUS.md` → *Buu Saga reference pass 1*), then brought **Priority 1, the 25th Tenkaichi Budokai, up to the reference beats** — Goku meeting Goten, the junior final played beat by beat (the pact, one hand behind Trunks' back, the sleeper hold, Goten's gold, the second hand, an engine-called ring-out), Spopovich's neck and Yamu calling him off, Gohan's Super Saiyan 2 held through and the siphon draining it — plus Vegeta's point-blank palms on Pui Pui (Priority 2). Priorities 3–6 already existed and were preserved; **priorities 7–13 (Fusion/Gotenks onward) are not built.** Now **61 missions** (55 canon, 6 What-If), **156** cutscenes, **70** characters (19 playable), **19** stages. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested — HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED**.

**Character-quality pass (2026-09-25):** only fixes in the Trunks vs Mecha Frieza finish (the vertical cut in the air with Trunks above Frieza and the blade through him; the blast from his empty hand in the air, no landing and no "End it" cue; a Super Saiyan with no hand signs) and the bald crown fixed in the hair construction for every character; **Videl, adult Gohan and Majin Vegeta rebuilt from the owner's reference collages** inside the existing character, wardrobe and form systems (bodies, faces, hair, outfits and story looks, clothing damage, their own poses); the Majin M now drawn on the skin for every marked character. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested — HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED**.

**The Future Trunks arrival, rebuilt (2026-09-24/25):** Chapter 5's first two missions rebuilt in place (same ids, order and unlocks) as one cinematic, playable arrival: *The Stranger* (the Time Machine, a playable massacre of Frieza's army with a finisher on every kill, Trunks' own Super Saiyan moment, a demonstration against Mecha Frieza whose mood runs denial → terrified, the Supernova caught, the cut, King Cold and the blade caught bare-handed) and *What He Came To Say* (Goku's pod, Instant Transmission and Roshi's sunglasses, the one-finger sword test, the future shown on a ruined-city stage, the medicine, the date drawn in the sand, the departure, THREE YEARS UNTIL THE ANDROIDS). Future Trunks redesigned from the owner's reference with his own sword kit and four wearable outfits. Now **61 missions** (55 canon, 6 What-If), **153** cutscenes, **70** characters (19 playable), **19** stages. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested — HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED**.

**The Difference in Power (2026-09-24):** the Cell Games' `purge` checkpoint (Super Saiyan 2 Gohan destroying the seven Cell Juniors) is now a hack-and-slash power reveal — same mission, same place in the story, no new mission id. Known Bugs #36 (a third Junior swinging at Gohan) is fixed at the root. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, **not human playtested — the owner's next step is to play it**.

**Customization (2026-09-24):** Goku, Vegeta and Bardock have a data-driven wardrobe; the canon story now dresses every mission in its era's look (Vegeta no longer wears Saiyan armor and a scouter on Namek, on Earth or in the Buu saga), and the player dresses them for Free Battle, Training and the menus from Main menu → Customization. Functional prototype, automated tested, browser-verified by Claude, not human playtested.

The owner decides when 0.3 is frozen into `builds/v0.3/`. The next version (0.4) is planned as the **Namek foundation**, continuing chronologically.

- **Trunks vs Mecha Frieza** (`frieza_finish` in `src/data/scenes/android_arrival.js`): Trunks reappears above Frieza, in line; an 18-frame cut drives the real blade (`K.bladeSegment`) down Frieza's axis; the halves part; the dicing flurry, the pieces (`K.dice`, group `frieza:pieces`) and the finish stay in the air — sword held back, empty left palm out (`ft_airPalm`), slow-motion and push-in while the ki gathers, the blast from the palm (`trunksFinalBlast` event), the pieces vaporised, a hero shot (`ft_airHero`); two cues now (Vanish, Cut). The transformation uses new still, fists-down poses (`ft_tfStill` … `ft_tfFinal`); the Burning Attack keeps `ft_burnW`.
- **Bald crown:** `setHair` never builds a cap smaller than `SKULL_COVER` (1.03 × 1.09 × 1.05 head radii) unless the style says `cap.partial` — Trunks, Bulma, Videl and adult Gohan had caps smaller than the skull.
- **Adult Gohan** (`adult_gohan.js` rewritten): adult build and face, `gohan_adult` hair with its own SSJ/SSJ2, outfits `ag_tournament` (canon), `ag_school` (story in *Seven Years*), `ag_ultimate` (outfit + hair only), a torn state, nine poses. **Videl** (`videl.js` rewritten): her build and face, pigtails with gold ties / short cut / bob, `videl_tee` (loose tee with a moving hem, bike shorts, fingerless gloves), `videl_tournament` (story in *Learning to Fly* and *Spopovich*), `videl_super`, damage that never strips the shirt, 20 poses. **Majin Vegeta** (`vegeta.js` form `majin`): `vegeta_majin` hair (high widow's-peak hairline), the M on the skin, under-eye creases, the `majin` sneer at rest (`form.restFace`), heavier limbs (`look.muscle`), his own stance, run, flight, guard, charge and laugh; the Buu suit's damage reaches the back and the glove.

Found and fixed there: the M reading as "| |" (strokes sunk into the skull, the SSJ2 hairline over the forehead) and Videl's hem opening at the sides. **Not human playtested.**

Wardrobe: `ft_capsule` (canon), `ft_capsule_worn`, `ft_capsule_torn`, `ft_capsule_unarmed`; three planned. New story characters: Bulma, Future Gohan, `kid_gohan_764` (Gohan at nine: Piccolo's gi, no hat, no tail).
- **Power gaps** (`SL.data.trunksArrival.GAP`): army ×7 dealt / ×0.08 taken; Mecha Frieza ×2.4 / ×0.05; the test against Goku has no multipliers — Goku's finger is the gap.

HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED** (checklist in `STATUS.md`).

On 2026-09-24 — **"The Difference in Power": Super Saiyan 2 Gohan vs the Cell Juniors, and Known Bugs #36 fixed** (owner's prompt with a reference sheet of SSJ2 Gohan dismantling the Juniors, plus a finisher-presentation note; *make the player feel how absurdly strong SSJ2 Gohan is; hack-and-slash, not a boss rush; show, don't tell; keep the Cinematic HUD; every kill a short anime finisher; do not rebuild systems; test the actual combat; browser-verify; update the documents; then stop*). Placement chosen by the owner when asked: **replace The Cell Games' `purge` phase** (it already was this beat), keeping the mission id, chronology, saves and results flow. `STATUS.md` → *The Difference in Power* has the full record.

- **Known Bugs #36, root cause:** the two-attacker cap was enforced through melee tokens, but a swing can reach Gohan without its Junior ever asking for one — a swing started on an ally (a combo press, a heavy, the throw-fallback heavy, a chase strike) was turned onto Gohan when the squad plan moved that Junior to circle him or its ally went down. Traced cases: seed 6 frame 933 (`cjSmash` begun on Goku, retargeted as a flanker) and seed 1 (three swings begun on Tien and Goku, all turned onto Gohan). **Fix:** the cap counts what is actually happening (`Battle.squadMeleeFull`: squad members in `attack` aimed at the player) and is enforced where every swing begins (`Fighter._startAttack`, which now returns whether it started; `_tryChase` before it spends ki) and where a swing is turned onto him (`AIHooks.cell_junior.pre` breaks off a turned swing when the quota is full). Squad members only (`f.squadRole`): Saibamen, Frieza soldiers and every other group fight are untouched. Also found while fixing it: a Junior sent to circle kept its melee token forever and could starve the pair meant to be on the player — circling Juniors now give their player token back. **Measured:** seeds 1–8 all hold at two (before: seeds 1 and 6 broke it); the Juniors test is back on seed 6, the comment removed; a regression test replays seeds 1 and 6.
- **The encounter** (`src/data/missions/cell_purge.js`, run by `P.purge` in `cell_games.js` with its staging helpers): Gohan on the ring's centre inside a ring of seven grinning Juniors (scene `purge_open`, no dialogue); the first blow that reaches him is caught in one hand, a beat of silence, and the Junior is punched off the ring (scene `purge_catch`, only if he has not already started destroying them); then the fight — the squad (two swinging at most, the rest circling and throwing Junior Blasts), moods `hunt` → `desperate` (quicker hand-overs, the circle climbs so the next one dives in) → `last` (the last two together), hesitation after every kill (each on its own beat), a step back after being smashed; behaviour only — the Juniors' pack frenzy is switched off here so losing a sibling never makes them stronger. Gohan: damage ×1.8, damage taken ×0.3, light blows land but do not move him (`storyArmor`), a blow from behind is felt — he turns, takes it on one arm (a quiet Perfect Guard) and counters by himself (`storyEvade` mode `guard`) — and an **Aura Burst** on the Ultimate button (the `bomberWave` technique: every Junior close by is launched outward). No objective text, no prompts.
- **Finishers:** every Junior he destroys gets a hit-stop on both of them, a short slow-down, lightning off him, and a body reaction chosen by the killing blow: *launch* (heavy blows — across the ring), *rocket* (the rising strike — straight up), *slam* (smashes and blows from above — into a crater), *spike* (light blows in the air — driven down at an angle), *blast* (ki — blown away), *burst* (the Aura Burst). The last one holds longest and slowest, then scene `purge_last`: dust at the body, Gohan coming down onto the ring, the camera slowly round him while the fallen crumble, Cell watching. The bodies stay where they fell until then.
- **World and gi:** his big blows crack the ring under a grounded Junior, bodies crater it (the existing crater/crack code), the burst scorches it; Gohan's gi goes clean → damaged (second kill) → heavily damaged (fifth kill) with its own rips (teen Gohan now has `outfit.damage`); the later checkpoints keep that damage, and Cell's return still tears the gi as before.
- **Shared systems changed (each with regression coverage):** `Battle.squadMeleeFull`, `Fighter._startAttack` return value (#36); `storyEvade` gains `mode: 'guard'`, `behind` and `pose`; `storyArmor`; `perfectEffects(…, quiet)`; the `ko` event carries the hit; `keepBody` (skip the 5-second body removal); `packHunter` gains `noFrenzy` and `witherAfter`; **`bomberWave` now costs its ki and starts its cooldown when pressed from a technique slot** (it was free and could be pressed again at once — Android 17's Energy Field, Android 16's Hell Flash Charge and Frieza's Death Wave as specials were affected; boss patterns are unchanged) and takes an optional `look` (no warning dome, smaller fireball/ring/light/dust, aura flare) and `impact` — defaults unchanged; the Junior squad hook gains moods (only when a mission sets `battle.juniorMood`).
- **Tests:** +15 in the new `tests/cell-purge-tests.js` (wired in from `sim-tests.js`); the Juniors test back on seed 6. Full suite: **663 passed, 0 failed** (648 before).
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (dev server, canvas captures, Cinematic HUD): the opening, the catch, the fight, sensed blows and counters, the Aura Burst, finishers, the worn gi, the closing scene and the hand-over to Gohan vs Cell; zero damage numbers in Cinematic, no console errors. Nine framing and white-out problems were found there and fixed (listed in `STATUS.md`). **Not human playtested. HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED — the owner's next step.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** no new mission id or duplicate mission; no HUD redesign; no Buu, Namek, What-If or wardrobe changes; no stat inflation for the Juniors; no external assets. `groundSlam` has the same free-from-a-slot problem `bomberWave` had (Known Bugs #38, open — not needed here).
- **Files created:** `src/data/missions/cell_purge.js`, `tests/cell-purge-tests.js`. **Files changed:** `src/data/missions/cell_games.js` (the `purge` phase, `prep`, `restoreStory`), `src/data/scenes/cell_games.js` (three scenes), `src/data/characters/teen_gohan.js` (poses `tg2_catch`, `tg2_block`, `tg2_glance`; gi damage), `src/data/characters/cell_junior.js` (pose `cj_caught`), `src/game/battle.js`, `fighter.js`, `controllers.js`, `techniques.js`, `mechanics.js`, `index.html`, `tests/run-tests.js`, `sim-tests.js`, `cell-games-tests.js`, and these documents.

On 2026-09-24 — **Manga Mode: the alternate Namek ending, kept as a secret** (owner's notes after seeing it: *keep the one it was, hide the other as a "manga mode" we expand later; the sad face belongs after he thinks he killed Frieza, and when Frieza fires he should turn slowly and fire before the beam reaches him*). `STATUS.md` -> *Manga Mode* has the record.

- **The ending plays the classic cut, unchanged.** `gameplay.namekEnding` still defaults to `classic`.
- The alternate cut is **hidden**: type **MANGA** on the Settings screen and the row "Manga Mode: Namek Ending" appears under Gameplay (toast: *SECRET FOUND*). The find is stored in `settings.secrets.manga`. The gate is generic — any schema row tagged `secret: '<name>'` hides until that flag is true, so the next Manga Mode piece is one row.
- The cut itself was re-timed: Goku never flinches or guards, the death beam is capped short of him, and he **turns slowly and fires straight out of the turn**, his wave going through the one still climbing at him. The **sad face is the last beat only** — a new `sad` expression in `src/game/model.js`, worn when he lands and looks down at the man he thinks he has killed.
- Both cuts still leave Frieza alive with `friezaSurvivedNamek`. 3 tests in `tests/playtest4-tests.js`; browser-verified by Claude; **not human playtested**.

On 2026-09-24 — **Wardrobe visual refinement + Bardock fix** (owner's prompts with a Goku reference sheet, two Bardock references and a screenshot of the stuck Bardock screen; *refinement only, do not rebuild the architecture, stop after this pass*). `STATUS.md` → *Wardrobe visual refinement + Bardock fix* has the full record (root cause, compatibility matrix, tails, the reference-driven changes, tests, browser checks, limitations, checklist).

- **Bardock bug, root cause:** outfits built on another with `base` inherited only its look, not its compatibility, so Bardock's damaged/movie/Super outfits refused their own default armor; and `sanitize`'s fallback re-picked that same armor without checking it fit, so `select` refused the result and the screen printed the internal reason. **Fix:** compatibility inherits through `base` (`trait()`), outfits can list allowed `armors`, every fallback is checked, the screen resolves dependent pieces silently, `validate` checks each outfit against its own defaults, and `Save.load` migrates invalid saved looks (`SL.Wardrobe.migrate`).
- **Tails:** the waist ring and the armor's seat plate (the "brown oval") are gone; a tail is now a real tail — *wrapped* (a tapered tube from the tailbone, one coil round the waist) or *free* (jointed, hangs and curls). Pieces `none` / `tail_saiyan` (wrapped) / `tail_saiyan_free`. Adult Goku never has one; Saiyan-era Vegeta and Bardock wear it wrapped.
- **Bardock:** classic/Z (dark armor, green straps, long red guards, headband, scar) and Super/Broly movie (big olive flaps, grey chest, blue suit and guards, white boots, no headband/scar) are two designs; the movie armor over the classic self stays as an alternate. New model fields: `padStyle: 'flap'`, `padScale`, `wristLen`; outfits can change the face (`face`).
- **Goku/Vegeta:** Yardrat rebuilt (lilac collar, white puffy sleeves, navy vest, white sash, red trousers), the "59" jacket rebuilt as an open layer over a shirt with the sleeve patch, Badman given a real collar. New model fields: `collar`/`collarStyle`, `jacket`, `sleeveBulk`, `beltStyle: 'sash'`, emblem `sleeve`. Everything else kept as it was.
- **Tests:** +7 in `tests/wardrobe-tests.js`. Full suite: **622 passed, 0 failed** (615 before).
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (the owner's Bardock sequence step by step, rear views of every Saiyan look, the refined outfits, the story override, reload). **Not human playtested. HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** no architecture rebuild, no new saga/boss/stage/What-If, no external images or assets in the game (the references were used as reference only).
- **Files changed:** `src/game/model.js`, `src/game/wardrobe.js`, `src/data/wardrobe.js`, `src/core/save.js`, `src/ui/screens_wardrobe.js`, `src/data/characters/kid_gohan.js`, `src/data/characters/cell.js`, `tests/wardrobe-tests.js`, and these documents.

The one-time shift of the seeded stream then exposed a **pre-existing mission bug**: in *Five Minutes* an eruption hitting Frieza ended the saucer phase and played "cut down by his own saucer" (fixed in `namek_end.js`, regression test added); three other seeded bot tests were re-measured across seeds (rates written into the tests: What-If Piccolo's optional team attack 3/6, *Junior Division* 2/6 — Known Bugs #20, Frieza's approach sample widened to eight seeds).

On 2026-09-23 — **First-launch visual experience setup / Display & Immersion** (owner's prompt; *presentation, HUD density and immersion only: no combat balance, story, save architecture or accessibility option removed; stop after this pass*). `STATUS.md` → *First-launch visual setup / Display & Immersion* has the full record (what Cinematic hides, what stays contextual, the migration, the tests, the browser checks, limitations, the checklist).

- **First launch:** after *Press any button*, while `visualExperienceConfigured` is false, the title goes to `SL.Screens.visualSetup` (`src/ui/screens_settings.js`) instead of the main menu: *"How do you want to experience Saiyan Legacy?"* over the menu backdrop (Goku and Vegeta in a stand-off, dust, a restless aura, quiet music), **Cinematic** (recommended, focused first) / **Standard** / **Custom**; focusing a choice rearranges a preview HUD made of the real HUD pieces. Custom opens Settings on Display & Immersion. It can be reopened from that page (*Experience Setup*).
- **Settings → Display & Immersion** (the old HUD tab, category `hud`): HUD Preset, Damage Numbers (Off / Important Only / All), Player and Enemy Health (Full / Minimal / Hidden), Ki and Stamina (Full / Minimal / Contextual), Lock-On UI (Full / Minimal / Contextual / Hidden), Skill UI (Full / Icons Only / Contextual / Hidden), Technique Names and Tutorial Tips (both moved here from Gameplay), Button Hints, Objective Display, Notifications, Combo Counter, Auto-Hide HUD, Subtitles (On / Dialogue Only / Cinematic Only / Off), UI Opacity, HUD Scale. Presets live in `SL.Settings.HUD_PRESETS`; a row change makes the preset Custom and keeps every value.
- **HUD** (`src/ui/hud.js`): `_wake(kind)` / `_awake(kind, hold)` / `_visibility()` — each element shows when it has a reason (hit, spend, charge, target change, a boss's new form, critical health) and Auto-Hide rests the rest after 4 quiet seconds. Minimal and contextual styles are CSS classes on the existing elements (`hp-min`, `ki-min`, `st-min`, `res-off`, `.hud-skills.min`, `.hud-objectives.min`, `.lock.min`). The pause menu lists the current objectives. Damage numbers no longer use `Math.random` (they changed a seeded fight's random stream).
- **Tips:** `Mission.prompt(..., { required: true })` shows in every mode; **43 story prompts** are marked required (struggles, the beams and forms a scene waits for, mission-specific mechanics, lethal floors). Tips Contextual: a learned hint never returns, a shown hint does not loop within a mission, and hints come one at a time (found stacked three deep in the browser). Subtitles: barks carry `kind: 'bark'` (`Battle.say`'s new 4th argument).
- **Saves:** the choice lives in the existing settings store (`saiyanLegacy.settings.v1`); older settings are migrated once (`Settings.migrateHud`) to the matching choices, usually Standard; progress is untouched; such players see the setup once.
- **Tests:** +15 in the new `tests/immersion-tests.js`, which attaches the real HUD and menus to headless battles through the new `tests/minidom.js`. Full suite: **600 passed, 0 failed** (585 before).
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (the setup, the three previews, the choice, and story fights under each preset). **Not human playtested. HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** no combat balance, story, What-If or Namek change; no new UI framework.
- **Files created:** `tests/immersion-tests.js`, `tests/minidom.js`. **Files changed:** `src/core/settings.js`; `src/ui/hud.js`, `screens_settings.js`, `screens_menu.js`, `screens_battle.js`; `src/game/mission.js`, `battle.js`; `css/game.css`; 23 mission files (the `required` flags); `tests/headless.js`, `tests/sim-tests.js`; and these documents.

On 2026-09-23 (earlier the same day) — **Frieza AI fairness / boss-feel pass** (owner's prompt; *Frieza still feels too strong; do not rebuild Namek, do not touch the What-Ifs; one focused pass, then stop for the human playtest*). `STATUS.md` → *Frieza AI fairness / boss-feel pass* has the full record (the measured sources, every change, the phase table, before/after numbers, the browser checks, limitations, the checklist).

- **Measured first, with a new tool:** `tools/frieza_probe.js` plays a Frieza phase with a human-scale player (sees him 18 frames late, reads tells, punishes openings) and reports tells, hit rates, openings, pressure chains, whiffs, distance, framing and health.
- **What made him oppressive:** a final-form Death Beam 10–13 frames from tell to impact that hit a reacting player 50–100 % of the time (and pierces guard); Death Saucers that re-aimed half-way to the target every frame (100 % hits); strings that chained on after a whiff; a dodged technique converted straight into a string; a 2-frame opener; the story power gap erasing punishes of his own recovery; a charge punished on the frame it began; a shaken Super Saiyan-phase Frieza who out-flew every approach (57 m) with hard-difficulty reflexes; and, found in the browser, a camera that could hide him behind Goku's head and a Kaioken aura that washed out everything behind it.
- **AI behaviour (shared, data-driven):** Death Beam raise → glint/aim commit (`aimLock`) → fire, 20–24-frame startups; saucers with a turn-rate limit (`seekTurn`); whiff recovery that ends the string (`whiffRecovery`, `ai.whiffBreath`); a slower opener only (`openerStartup`); a breath after each technique (`ai.patternBreath`); readable approach (`ai.approachBoost`); glowing ki volleys (`ai.blastTell`); a soft leash (`ai.leash`); charge punishment from the delayed view; `recover` no longer story-evadable.
- **Camera (shared, presentation only):** the rendered camera slides sideways when a locked target is hidden behind the player (`CameraRig.slide`; the logical camera gameplay reads is unchanged, so the simulation is identical); the player's own aura is drawn at 45 % under the gameplay camera, full strength in cinematic shots.
- **Namek phases tuned separately:** toying emperor (rhythm 2/100, short strings, ×0.5) → Kaioken (3/80, ×0.6 → 0.8) → x10 (3/80, ×0.6) → fifty percent (4/55, ×1.15) → Super Saiyan (power-shift package unchanged, player's difficulty instead of forced hard, short strings) → 100 % (unchanged: hard, 4/45). Frieza's health untouched everywhere; Free Battle Frieza keeps his numbers.
- **Tests:** +12 in the new `tests/frieza-fair-tests.js`; one existing Frieza test updated (it scored a charge the AI had not yet seen). Full suite: **585 passed, 0 failed** (573 before).
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (six fights, canvas captures): early Frieza, Kaioken x2–x4 and x10, fifty percent vs x20, Super Saiyan, 100 % on the dying planet. **Not human playtested. HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** no Namek rebuild, no What-If changes, no new saga, no combat redesign.
- **Files created:** `tools/frieza_probe.js`, `tests/frieza-fair-tests.js`. **Files changed:** `src/game/fighter.js`, `controllers.js`, `projectiles.js`, `techniques.js`, `battle.js`; `src/render/camera.js`, `vfx.js`; `src/data/characters/frieza_force.js`; `src/data/missions/namek_goku.js`, `namek_kaioken20.js`, `namek_super_saiyan.js`; `tests/sim-tests.js`; and these documents.

On 2026-09-23 (earlier the same day) — **What-If implementation pass** (owner's prompt; *use Astra's Bible, build a small polished batch before the human Namek playtest*). `STATUS.md` → *What-If implementation pass* has the full record.

Four branches were built from `planning/ASTRA_WHAT_IF_BIBLE.md`, each one a single mission that reuses existing characters, stages, combat and the existing mission cinematic system:

- **`whatif_tail`** — *"What if Goku never let go?"* (Bible S01), branches from *A Brother From Space*: the tail hold is a struggle that can be lost, the player then steers the held Raditz into Piccolo's firing line and finishes as Piccolo. Goku lives; Gohan never wakes.
- **`whatif_prince`** — *"What if Vegeta refused the moon?"* (Bible S27), branches from *The Saiyan Prince*: **played as Vegeta**, he crushes his own Power Ball, fights an exhausted base-form duel and wins it by countering the Kaioken rush. Krillin and Gohan stop the killing blow; he leaves with the debt unpaid.
- **`whatif_signal`** — *"What if the warning got out?"* (Bible P03), branches from *The Final Stand*: a defend-the-relay encounter in orbit — hold a broadcast open for thirty seconds against a communications squad. He still dies; the fragment does not.
- **`whatif_twenty`** — *"What if x20 never became a Kamehameha?"* (Bible N24), branches from *Kaio-ken Times Twenty*: short x20 bursts against Frieza's firing positions, then the aura dies mid-air and the mission **hands the player Piccolo** over a helpless Goku. It ends in a retreat with Frieza alive and hurt.

Canon was not touched: no canon mission, ending or save record changed, the branches live on the separate What-If save track (`Save.isWhatIf`), each unlocks only when its own canon mission is cleared, and the results screen returns to the What If? list. No engine changes and no new cutscene files were needed. **+17 tests** in the new `tests/whatif-tests.js`; every branch browser-verified by Claude; **none of it human playtested**.

On 2026-09-23 (later the same day) — **Namek finale QA and cinematic polish** (owner's prompt; *continue from round 4, do not rebuild it; make the result feel like the Frieza finale, and finish the story*). `STATUS.md` → *Namek Finale QA + Cinematic Polish (round 5)* has the finding-by-finding record.

- **Baseline before the pass:** 544 passed, 0 failed. **After: 552 passed, 0 failed** (+8 in `tests/playtest4-tests.js`, now 30 tests).
- **The ending exists now, and it is the canon one.** *Five Minutes* runs DYING → HUNDRED → FULL → KRILLIN → FINISH → **SAUCERS** → **MERCY** → **BETRAYAL** → ESCAPE → AFTER. The fight breaks Frieza but never kills him; his own Death Saucer does the cutting (the engine's existing "a saucer past its leash turns on everybody" rule is the counterplay, and the phase is played); he begs; Goku answers him, walks away and gives him the energy to leave; he fires at Goku's back; "YOU FOOL!!"; a **gold one-handed Kamehameha** the player pushes, filmed on Frieza rather than on a white screen. He is left buried and **alive** — Chapter 5 already opens with him rebuilt at Earth.
- **The transformation reads as a progression**: Frieza goes confidence → confusion → disbelief → shock → anger, and **Goku sends Gohan off the planet with Bulma before he changes**, not after.
- **Two Krillin beats**: the quiet realization ("You're talking about Krillin, don't you?") in the Super Saiyan mission, and the shout on the dying planet against 100 % Frieza with the gi already gone.
- **Health floors**: Goku ends the Kaioken and Spirit Bomb missions at 25–26 %, hurt and standing, instead of on one hit point.
- **The dying planet is dressed**: craters cut into the real terrain, broken slabs, and vents that keep smoking through a new `stage.ambient` hook; base light up and mission mood down so it reads as burning rather than dark.
- **New engine pieces (small):** `Model.setHalved`, `fighter.auraOff`, `stage.ambient[]`.
- **Browser-verified by Claude** across the whole climax in order, with camera fixes found there (the fallen Frieza filmed from above, his aura still burning, the ruin too dark, the Kamehameha hiding its target, a standing pose on a body with no legs).
- **Nothing is human playtested.** The owner paused playtesting for the duration of the implementation pass and will play the Namek sequence next. **HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** Majin Vegeta / Milestone C, the Original Dragon Ball bridge, any What-If work (`planning/ASTRA_WHAT_IF_BIBLE.md` untouched), and every saga after Namek.
- **Files created:** `src/data/scenes/namek_finale.js`. **Files changed:** `src/game/model.js`, `fighter.js`, `stage.js`; `src/data/stages.js`; `src/data/missions/namek_end.js`, `namek_kaioken20.js`, `namek_spirit_bomb.js`; `src/data/scenes/namek_super_saiyan.js`, `namek_spirit_bomb.js`, `namek_kaioken20.js`; `tools/mission_brains.js`; `tests/sim-tests.js`, `tests/playtest4-tests.js`; `index.html`, `tests/run-tests.js`; and these documents.

On 2026-09-23 — **Human Playtest Round 4: the Namek final Frieza rebuild** (owner's prompt; *rebuild the post-burial Goku vs Frieza stretch as paced playable missions; do not start Majin Vegeta, the Original Dragon Ball bridge or any What-If work*). `STATUS.md` → *Human Playtest Round 4* has the full record (structure, tests, browser verification, limitations, the human checklist).

- **Baseline before the pass:** 522 passed, 0 failed. **After: 544 passed, 0 failed** (+22 in `tests/playtest4-tests.js`; the Namek climax suites in `sim-tests.js` and two assertions in `playtest3-tests.js` were rewritten for the new structure).
- **The climax is six missions**, alternating cinematic and gameplay: *The Prince's Last Words* → *The Emperor's True Power* (base Goku, then Kaioken x2→x4) → ***Kaio-ken Times Twenty*** (new: x10, Frieza's fifty percent, x20, the x20 Kamehameha as a player-driven struggle) → *The Spirit Bomb* (**played as Piccolo** while Goku gathers) → *The Legendary Super Saiyan* (Krillin, the transformation, the reversal) → *Five Minutes* (the dying planet, 100 % Frieza, the earned torn gi, the late Krillin line).
- **New content:** `src/data/missions/namek_kaioken20.js` and `src/data/scenes/namek_kaioken20.js` (4 scenes), `false_victory` / `frieza_returns` / `piccolo_takes_the_beam` in the Spirit Bomb scenes, `goku_legend`, `namek_dying`, `frieza_full_power`, and the `namek_ruin` stage (lava where the water was).
- **Engine additions (small, compatible):** `Model.cutTail` / `Model.setBattered`; a real tail on Frieza's appearance (he had none); Frieza's story-locked **100 %** form with its own transformation sequence; `fighter.aiPhase` (`dominant` / `shaken` / `full`) read by `AIHooks.frieza`; `fighter.barkOverrides` so a losing boss stops taunting; a mission-side **poise ceiling** for the post-transformation power shift.
- **Checkpoints on every major beat**: the death, the burial, both Kaioken fights, the x10 and x20 fights, the Kamehameha, the gathering, Frieza's return, Piccolo's sacrifice, Krillin, the transformation, 100 % and the escape. Each one restages its own cast, so entering it directly looks like playing into it.
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (12 sequences, canvas captures). Camera work fixed in the browser: pod occlusion, head-frame cameras on kneeling/lying actors, the cliff, the Kamehameha's beam hiding Frieza, ally auras in the silence, a missing Piccolo.

On 2026-09-21 — **Human Playtest Round 3: the Namek climax** (owner's prompt; *fix the Ultimate interruption, the blocking, the arrival, Vegeta's death, the HUD form and Frieza's rhythm; do not start Milestone C*). `STATUS.md` → *Human Playtest Round 3* has the finding-by-finding record.

- **Baseline before the pass:** 507 passed, 0 failed. **After: 522 passed, 0 failed** (+15 in `tests/playtest3-tests.js`).
- **Committed ultimates** (`Fighter.committedUltimate` / `ultimateArmor`): past its startup an ultimate is braced — ordinary hits land and hurt 35 % more, a beam, a throw or an ultimate-class blow still stops it, a Spirit Bomb braces against ordinary blows only. The AI no longer reacts to a technique before it could see it, and stops attacking one it can no longer interrupt. Measured 1/6 → 6/6 (Piccolo), 0/6 → 5/6 (Gohan), 2/6 → 5/6 (Goku at range).
- **AI fight rhythm** (`def.ai.rhythm`, `aiBreath`): bursts of pressure with announced breathing room; Frieza 3 attacks then ~1.2 s. Clear openings per minute 2.6 → 3.8.
- **Blocking helpers** `Cinema.twoShot` and `Cinema.overShoulder`: a shot built from where the actors stand. "Then do stand still" is now said to Vegeta's face, with a test that both are inside the camera frustum.
- **Form chronology** (`SL.data.formGivenBy` + `Mission._lockUnearnedForms`): no canon mission can offer a transformation the story has not handed over — the HUD said "Super Saiyan" during the Kaioken because three missions overwrote `lockedForms`. Audited across all 50 canon missions.
- **Kaioken steps**: x2 / x3 / x4 as before, plus **x10** (Frieza) and **x20** (the Spirit Bomb), handed over by the missions that own them; a per-form `defaultLevelCap` keeps them out of Free Battle and off the skill wheel elsewhere.
- **Canon order**: the landing moved into *The Prince's Last Words* (landing → protect him → the death → the last words → the burial → the declaration), and *The One They Were Waiting For* is now the fight that follows it.
- **New scenes**: `frieza_kills_vegeta` (the prince speaks, Frieza answers, the beam crosses the gap, he is thrown, he falls, Goku's face) and `goku_declares_frieza` (the owner's twelve-line speech, performed). The arrival scene was rebuilt beat by beat.
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (8 checks, including the HUD reading "Kaioken x2" with `ssj` locked, and a committed Final Flash taking four hits before it fires). **Nothing from this pass is human playtested.**
- **Not done, by instruction:** Milestone C (**NOT STARTED**); the Original Dragon Ball chronology pass (**NOT STARTED**).
- **Files created:** `tests/playtest3-tests.js`. **Files changed:** `src/game/fighter.js`, `controllers.js`, `cinema.js`, `mission.js`, `techniques.js`; `src/data/story.js`, `poses_common.js`, `src/data/characters/frieza_force.js`; `src/data/scenes/namek_arrival.js`, `namek_vegeta_end.js`, `namek_kaioken.js`; `src/data/missions/namek_frieza1.js`, `namek_vegeta_end.js`, `namek_goku.js`; `tests/sim-tests.js`; and the four documents.

On 2026-09-18 — **the Human Playtest / Core Feel / Camera Polish pass** (owner's prompt; *do not start Milestone C yet*). `STATUS.md` → *Human Playtest / Core Feel / Camera Polish pass* has the full record (COMPLETED, TESTED, BROWSER VERIFIED, HUMAN PLAYTESTED, remaining issues, future work, the owner's checklist).

- **Baseline before the pass:** `node tests/run-tests.js` — 462 passed, 0 failed. **After: 480 passed, 0 failed** (+18 in `tests/feel-tests.js`).
- **Raditz What-If beam duel** (`saiyan_raditz.js`): Raditz reacts instead of running a timer; his charge **flares** to open the window; an early release is graded weaker but is still a winnable struggle, a late one gets Goku's beam forced out point-blank; he always answers (no free kill); a lost struggle restages and only the third loss fails; the stand-off is framed side-on so the flare is visible.
- **Camera** (`camera.js`, `input.js`, `battle.js`): moving the mouse right now turns right (it was inverted), no drift, **Invert Horizontal**; modes **Combat** (locked), **Adventure** (out of fights and in traversal, eases toward the run), **Free** (never auto-turns); **Settings → Camera Style** Auto / Adventure / Free; *Snake Way* runs in Adventure.
- **Discoverability** (`hud.js`): a small **TARGET LOCKED · Tab Free Camera / FREE CAMERA · Tab Lock Target** chip that fades once learned; a first-flight hint with the real bindings; Settings labels **Lock-On / Free Camera**, **Jump / Fly Up (Ascend)**, **Fly Down / Land (Descend)**.
- **Far environmental targets** (`targeting.js`, `projectiles.js`): props marked `far` are lockable by Tab / Next Target / free aim and shots fly all the way to them; the moon in *Alone in the Wild* is one — only a shot that reaches it destroys it.
- **Skill wheel** shows each technique's target mode (→ target, → where you aim, all around, self).
- **Charge mixing** (`Battle.chargeLoop` / `_mixCharges`): primary charges keep full voice, secondaries fade and duck, a crowd of identical charges keeps two voices, transformations outrank charging; secondary aura particles thin (`emitMul`).
- **Father-Son Kamehameha**: a five-beat scene ending on Goku's **spirit** (translucent, halo, intangible, untargetable, no brain) at Gohan's back for the whole struggle, the voice insert, a slowed push-through, the spirit fading with the beam.
- **Bardock's death**: armor fractures and glows, the shoulder guards and chest plate break away, a face close-up with the faint smile, then he is thrown back through the light until it takes him (beats `armorBreak`, `blownAway`).
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (13 checks, including *The Mark* rendered for the first time); three problems found there and fixed. **Nothing has been human playtested.**

On 2026-09-18 — **the Buu Saga, Milestone B: the World Tournament and the wizard** (owner's Milestone B prompt). `STATUS.md` → *The Buu Saga — Milestone B* has the piece-by-piece record, the adaptation notes, the costume/form decision, the architecture audit and the human checklist.

- **Baseline before Milestone B:** `node tests/run-tests.js` — 438 passed, 0 failed.
- **Systems:** `SL.Tournament` (`src/game/tournament.js`: data bracket, playable match with ring-out / knockout / judges' decision, reusable announcer beat, bracket card); **ring-out as an engine rule** (`rules.ringOut`, never while a scene holds the game; Free Battle on the new `tournament` stage plays by it); **learned flight** (`Fighter.canFly`, per-fighter `flightCtl`: lift, speed, boost, wobble, ceiling, stamina drain, stall and recovery; `flightUnlock` on a definition); **petrification** (a `stone` hit, mash to break, stone materials); Yakon's absorber **overload**; a guard so a fighter is never knocked out twice.
- **Eight missions**: Chapter 8 *Learning to Fly* (Videl), *Junior Division* (Kid Trunks), *Spopovich* (Videl), *The Supreme Kai* (Gohan); Chapter 9 *The Pursuit* (Gohan), *Planet Zoon* (Vegeta), *The Dark Planet* (Goku), *The Demon King* (Gohan) — which ends with Babidi recalling Dabura because he has found Vegeta, the hand-off to *The Mark*.
- **Babidi is a system, not a fighter**: he watches every floor fight from a rock above it and answers what the player does (`watch()` in `src/data/missions/buu_ship.js`, event `babidiWatch`), and he ends the last fight himself.
- **New cast (Level 1):** Mr. Satan, Idasa, Ikose, the Supreme Kai, Kibito, Babidi, Dabura (**playable after *The Demon King***), Spopovich, Yamu, Pui Pui, Yakon. **New stages:** `tournament`, `babidi_crater`, `ship_zoon`, `ship_dark`, `demon_realm`.
- **Fixed on the way:** the Milestone A fighters could never be unlocked for Free Battle (unlock rules added for them, Satan City, the tournament stage and Dabura); every rush special without a `dash` pose and every scatter special whose release pose is called `fire` played an undefined pose — this affected older characters too (Nail, Android 18, Ginyu, Burter, Recoome, Mecha Frieza, King Cold, Androids 17 and 19); the fast test loader did not list any Buu-era file.
- **Tests:** `tests/buu-b-tests.js`, **16 tests** (bracket, announcer, ring-out, every match path, Videl's flight stages, the cast, form locks, story order and locks, save/load, unlocks, **checkpoint safety across nine checkpoints**, every Buu mission **watched in full with no warnings**, the pursuit's three failure modes, watched/skipped parity for the ship arrival, Babidi's reactions, Yakon's overload, Dabura's recall); `tests/headless.js` now collects warnings and can play every scene in full.
- **Browser-verified by Claude** (canvas captures, because the pane was not drawing): every mission and cinematic; thirteen problems found there and fixed (listed in `STATUS.md`). **Nothing has been human playtested.**

On 2026-09-18 — **the Buu Saga, Milestone A: seven years later** (owner's Buu continuation prompt; the saga is planned as milestones A–J, see `STATUS.md` → *The Buu Saga — Milestone A* for the plan and the gap report).

On 2026-09-17 — **The Cell Games, rebuilt as interactive anime** (owner directive; this replaces the ten-phase version described further down). `STATUS.md` has the piece-by-piece record; the short version:

- **The mission is now 28 phases and 11 checkpoints** (`src/data/missions/cell_games.js`), playing the whole sequence: the ring and the opening → **Goku vs Cell in four escalating playable stages** (testing on the ground, serious with flight and beams, an aerial exchange, and Cell's Kamehameha at the planet, which has to be answered) → Cell regenerates, Goku is spent, forfeits and throws him a Senzu Bean → **Gohan in base form**, then Super Saiyan, then refusing → 16's failed self-destruct → **the Cell Juniors** set on the Z Fighters → **Android 16's death** → **Super Saiyan 2** → the Juniors destroyed → Super Saiyan 2 vs a Cell who **adapts** → 18 spat out, Semi-Perfect Cell → **Goku's sacrifice** → **Cell's return** (Trunks killed, Vegeta broken, Gohan's arm) → **the playable Father-Son Kamehameha beam struggle** → Cell's defeat → the aftermath. Every cinematic beat is entered from the state of the fight (a flag, a health threshold, a phase), never from a clock.
- **17 scenes** (`src/data/scenes/cell_games.js`), all played by the mission. Dialogue is paraphrased English written for this project; **no Japanese was added anywhere**, because none was verified.
- **The Father-Son Kamehameha is played, not watched.** It is also kept for its moment: before the last stand the ultimate slot holds an ordinary full-power Kamehameha, so the story never fires the famous beam early. In the last stand Cell charges above the ring; Gohan's answer stays locked until Cell's beam is actually out, so the struggle cannot be skipped; the beams meet and the player pushes; his father's voice comes when he is pushed back; Vegeta's blast lands when he pushes past the middle; winning the struggle is what kills Cell, and every point of Cell's health is accounted for as real damage.
- **Android 16's death** is staged as the owner asked: his line, silence with the music and the auras gone, the tears, the shadow, the red line across the face, the ring cracking and rocks lifting, then the scream and the transformation. Watched or skipped, it leaves the same state.
- **Super Saiyan 2 is a different fighter**, not a multiplier: its own stance, shorter and meaner strings, a guard-folding gut punch, a blink that ends behind you, Perfect-Guard counters, Super Kamehameha, Unleashed Rush, an aggressive CPU profile, its own intro and victory, and permanent lightning.
- **The Cell Juniors are a squad**: at most two work the player at once and they rotate, nobody dogpiles or stun-locks, the rest are spread over the Z Fighters at most two per ally, and the allies really are beaten.
- **New cinematic mission *The Fever*** (`android_fever`, Chapter 5, between *Ten in the Morning* and *The Laboratory Door*): Goku down with the heart virus and Chi-Chi beside him, on a new interior stage `son_house`, with a new story-only character `chichi`. **Chronology note:** canon puts Goku in that bed during the Android saga, not during the Cell Games, so it is its own mission there. It is data-driven; if the owner wants it inside the Cell Games instead, it is a scene move, not a rewrite.
- **Engine work it needed:** a form can carry its own poses, moves (including counters), specials, AI profile, intro and victory; `BeamClash.maxSeconds` for story clashes; `Battle.on` returns an unsubscribe and `Battle.off` exists; Cinema events `rage` and `crack`; the HUD skill panel reads the active form's techniques.
- **Tests:** `tests/cell-games-tests.js` — **18 tests** wired into the suite (they replaced three outdated ones).
- **Browser-verified by Claude, beat by beat** (arena, all four Goku stages, the forfeit, Gohan's path, the Juniors, 16's death with the rebuilt rage visual, the Super Saiyan 2 reveal, the payoff fight, Cell's escalation, the beam struggle with the HUD readable, the defeat, the aftermath, the bed scene, a checkpoint restart; no console errors). Thirteen framing, staging and gameplay problems were found there and fixed — including that the beam struggle could be skipped by firing early. **Nothing here has been human playtested.**
- **Status: FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE · AUTOMATED TESTED · BROWSER-VERIFIED BY CLAUDE · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED.**

On 2026-09-17 — **Chapter 7 opens: the Buu saga**:

- **Chapter 7 · *The Mark*** (`src/data/missions/buu_majin.js`, played as Goku; phases `offer, duel, price, answer, hatch, after`, checkpoints `duel` and `answer`). OFFER (scene `majin_offer`: a wizard offers Vegeta what seven years of being second could not give him, and the scene's point is that he is not possessed so much as given permission) → **DUEL** (checkpoint: thirty-five seconds of a tournament match that is not about winning — Vegeta is on infinite health and Goku's damage is held down) → PRICE (scene `majin_price`: the blast into the stands, and the sound dropping out) → ANSWER (checkpoint: the multipliers invert, damage counts, and Goku stops pulling them) → HATCH (scene `buu_hatches`) → AFTER.
- **Vegeta's third form, `majin`** (`src/data/characters/vegeta.js`): story-locked so the transformation wheel never offers it, ×1.75 damage, its own red aura and tint, and a 168-frame staged sequence.
